# Skills · S · part 180

864 entries, skill-autoresearch to skill-clash. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **skill-autoresearch**: Karpathy's autoresearch loop, reproduced faithfully and made reusable: an agent edits ONE artifact, runs an IMMUTABLE evaluator under a FIXED budget, reads ONE scalar metric, keeps the commit if it improved and reverts if not, logs every experiment to results.tsv, and never stops to ask. A program file (programs/*.md, human-edited) points the loop at an artifact, an evaluator and a metric. Ships two programs: output-style (Your Agent's chat voice, blind-judged against frozen references) and skill-clarity (a SKILL.md, scored by an executor's per-step confusion). Use it to improve any artifact… ([Pvragon/ai-workspace-reference](https://github.com/Pvragon/ai-workspace-reference/tree/HEAD/team-lib/skills/skill-autoresearch) · ★3)
- **skill-autoresearch**: Improve a Codex skill through bounded autoresearch: freeze a mechanical evaluator, record a baseline, make one focused change per iteration, keep only measured improvements, and preserve experiment evidence. Use when asked to autoresearch, optimize, tune, or systematically improve a SKILL.md, skill runtime, trigger boundary, or skill eval. The bundled runner is written in Rust. ([decebal/decebal-codex-skills](https://github.com/decebal/decebal-codex-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/skill-autoresearch) · ★2)
- **skill-autoresearch-loop**: Run a bounded autoresearch loop to improve one existing skill at a time using a fixed small harness, an explicit metric, and keep-or-reject iteration decisions. Use when ratcheting skill quality without turning the process into open-ended experimentation. - **skill-claim-check**: 招牌实测 ClaimCheck —— 给任何号称「让 AI 少写代码 / 更便宜 / 更快」的编程类 skill 或规则文件做一次祛魅实测：补上它最容易缺的正确性门槛，把「注入说明书的输入成本」和「省下来的输出」拆开算，得出诚实指标「每个正确答案到底花多少钱」，再按场景分清它到底省钱还是倒贴。全程走 Claude Code 订阅、headless 读真实 token，零 API key 即可复现。适用场景：看到某 skill README 写着「80% less code / 50% cheaper / 3x faster」想验真假；想用公允指标对比两套 system prompt 或两个 skill；想知道一个精简类 skill 在你的任务上值不值得长期开着。触发词：「实测这个skill」「这个skill的招牌数字是真的吗」「帮我给这个skill祛魅」「省代码是真的吗」「benchmark一下这个skill」「claim check」「verify this skill claims」「正确性门槛」「每个正确答案的成本」「cost per correct」。本工具是实测方法论，结论取决于你选的任务与模型，不构成对被测 skill 的最终评判。 ([fguo7370/skill-claim-check](https://github.com/fguo7370/skill-claim-check/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
